# Deferred Work Log

## Deferred from: 14-s4-ship-family-first-day-view (2026-07-30, dev-story)

- **D-14S4-1: Cooked / prepped signals have no persistence** — "Mark cooked" and "Done prepping" (and "Skip prep tonight") are part of the locked day-detail action vocabulary but no endpoint records them, so they ship disabled with an honest hint. Needs a `plan_day` completion signal (table + route + optimistic mutation) — and it is the natural source for the *familiarity* signal below. [`apps/web/src/features/day-detail/components/WallCardSwipeStack.tsx` ModeActionBar]
- **D-14S4-2: A paused day cannot be resumed from the UI** — `PlansRepository.unpauseDayById` exists (`plans.repository.ts:418`) but no HTTP route exposes it (verified against every registered `/v1/` path). The day view therefore renders a paused day view-only. Fix: a `DELETE`/`PATCH …/days/:day/pause` mirror of the pause route, then re-enable the action. [`apps/api/src/modules/plans/plans.routes.ts`]
- **D-14S4-3: No familiarity signal, so recipe-vs-method gating cannot work** — the locked rule says Method is shown only when the cook doesn't know the recipe; nothing in the data records that, so the method always expands and the card prints no familiarity claim (`familiarityKnown` is left undefined). Derive it from cook-count / `household_recipe_usage` once D-14S4-1 lands. [`apps/web/src/features/day-detail/day-view-model.ts`]
- **D-14S4-4: `prepInvestment` ("Sunday prep saved you 14 min") is mock-only** — no data source records what weekend prep saved, so the live projection never sets it and the Wall Card's rollup line self-hides. Revisit with D-14S4-1's timing data. [`apps/web/src/features/day-detail/day-view-model.ts`]
- **D-14S4-5: `/alpha` modifiers are dead on EVERY semantic token, repo-wide** — verified against the compiled CSS: no alpha-modified utility on a `var()`-backed token is generated at all (`border-border/30`, `text-fg-muted/80`, `bg-surface/60`, `bg-bg/90`, `text-fg/90`, `bg-amber-warm/10`…). Borders silently fall back to `currentColor`; "muted" text just inherits. Fixed inside `features/day-detail` by this story; the rest of the app still carries them. Needs a repo-wide sweep plus the `--amber-warm-fill`-style token work already tracked as D-14S3-CR4. [repo-wide; see also `packages/design-system/tokens/colors.css`]

## Deferred from: code review of 14-s3-elevate-the-week (2026-07-30)

- **D-14S3-CR1: Saturday row interactive but tap/Enter silently no-ops** — 6-day plans render Saturday with full swap affordance (cursor-pointer, tabIndex=0, Enter handler), but `summonForDay` early-returns because `FULL_TO_SHORT` has no `saturday` key; the user gets no feedback. Pre-existing (identical in the old tile grid). Fix: gate `onSwapIntent` on `FULL_TO_SHORT[day] !== undefined` in BriefContent, or extend the plan-edit day vocabulary to saturday. [`apps/web/src/features/plan/useBriefView.ts:15-21`, `BriefContent.tsx:summonForDay`]
- **D-14S3-CR2: `aria-label` on role-less `<div>` week containers** — Naming a `generic` element is undefined per ARIA; both the Brief week container ("Weekly plan") and PlanHistoryPage ("Historical weekly plan") do it. Pre-existing pattern, pinned by 13-s1 `getByLabel('Weekly plan')`. Fix needs a coordinated `role`/element change + lockstep test edits. [`apps/web/src/features/plan/WeekGrid.tsx:53`, `PlanHistoryPage.tsx:110`]
- **D-14S3-CR3: Inner interactive elements in the clickable day-row article lack a propagation/key guard** — `PlanTile.handleKeyDown` + article `onClick` fire `onSwapIntent` with no "ignore inner interactive target" check; "Why this?"/variant pills each hand-roll `stopPropagation` but "Editing locked" and "Pause Lunch Link" don't (currently unreachable in interactive states — frozen disables the tile; no Brief consumer passes `onPauseLunchLink`). This is also the blocker for relocating PackerChip into the row's status column. Fix: a single `(e.target as Element).closest('button,a')` guard in the article handlers. [`apps/web/src/features/plan/PlanTile.tsx`]
- **D-14S3-CR4: Variant-proposal pill violates the Honey rule on hover** — `hover:bg-amber-warm/20` on "Try the variant" contradicts DESIGN.md §4 (proposal hover = `--lumi-terracotta-warmed`); sibling of the already-deferred `--amber-warm`-hex `/alpha` broken-tint defect (today-morning tint computes to transparent). Fix both together with an `--amber-warm-fill`-style token slice. [`apps/web/src/features/plan/PlanTile.tsx` pending-input block, `packages/design-system/tokens/colors.css:151`]

## Deferred from: code review of 13-s11-route-collapse-4-anchors (2026-07-03)

- **D-13S11-CR1: `aria-current="page"` absent on Brief NavLink at artifact URLs** — `isBriefActive` drives only the CSS class; NavLink's own `isActive` (false at artifact URLs) controls `aria-current`. Screen readers don't get a current-page signal on `/app/grocery-list`, `/app/day/:day`, etc. Not WCAG-AA, but a meaningful a11y gap. Fix: pass explicit `aria-current` prop to Brief NavLink based on `isBriefActive`. [`apps/web/src/components/AppSidebar.tsx:66-67`]
- **D-13S11-CR2: Dual Dialog Escape listeners conflict when LumiSheet summoned + ArtifactSheet both open** — If `presenceState === 'summoned'` while on an artifact URL, both `LumiSheet` and `ArtifactSheet` register `document.keydown` handlers; Escape fires both, closing both dialogs and navigating to `/app`. No in-app path reaches this state post-P1-fix (P1 prevents stale summon). Needs Dialog stack/z-index coordination for correctness. [`apps/web/src/components/Dialog.tsx`]
- **D-13S11-CR3: `setContext` clears `turns: []` on every mount; turns flash empty when navigating artifact URLs while LumiSheet open** — `useLumiContext` calls `setContext` on new component instance; `setContext` always resets `turns`. With artifact pattern, navigating from `/app` to `/app/grocery-list` creates a new `AppHomePage` instance even though `surface` stays `brief`. Add a guard: skip the `turns: []` reset when `signal.surface === state.surface`. [`apps/web/src/hooks/useLumiContext.ts:18`, `apps/web/src/stores/lumi.store.ts:setContext`]
- **D-13S11-CR4: Voice session not torn down when navigating away from `/app/lumi`** — `LumiPage` starts a voice session but has no unmount cleanup calling `endSession()`. The WebSocket continues capturing audio on other routes. Voice lifecycle is managed at layout level (VoiceSessionProvider) and governed by 5-S5 voice spec; that story should add a route-change teardown hook. [`apps/web/src/routes/(app)/lumi.tsx`]
- **D-13S11-CR5: ArtifactSheet close button occludes hosted screen top-right content** — `absolute right-4 top-4 z-10` close button floats over hosted screens not designed for it. No padding/clearance added to children. Screens are frozen dev-mock routes; address when each screen is redesigned for the artifact context. [`apps/web/src/components/ArtifactSheet.tsx:43-49`]

## Deferred from: code review of 13-s2.5-sse-push-completion (2026-07-02)

- **D-13S2.5-CR1: Stage emit ordering (guardrail/persisting before commit)** — `guardrail` emits after `planWeek()` but before `commit()` (which runs the actual allergen check); `persisting` also fires before commit. Both are best-effort boundary markers — commit() bundles guardrail+persist atomically so there is no clean split point. Acknowledged in dev notes. Misleading during a guardrail-triggered regen cycle. [`apps/api/src/jobs/plan-generation.job.ts:564-592`]
- **D-13S2.5-CR2: `isRegenerating` renders hardcoded copy, not pushed stage** — While composing empty-state renders `planProgressLabel(stage)`, the regenerating path shows a fixed string unaffected by `plan.progress` events. Spec AC3 targets the draft spinner in the empty-state composing flow; regen copy update is ambiguous scope. [`apps/web/src/features/plan/BriefCanvas.tsx`]
- **D-13S2.5-CR3: Stale replayed `failed` event race** — A reconnect after a prior failed compose can replay the old `failed` event after `reset()` but before the new job's `composing` event arrives, tripping the error state for a fresh compose. Fix requires a nonce/generation counter in `plan-progress.store` so events from a previous job are ignored. [`apps/web/src/stores/plan-progress.store.ts`, `BriefCanvas.tsx`]

## Deferred from: code review of 13-s3-whisper-channel (2026-06-29)

- **D-13S3-CR1: `aria-controls` references sheet element that may not be in DOM** — `LumiPresence.tsx`: `aria-controls="lumi-sheet"` is set unconditionally but the sheet element is conditionally rendered; dangling `aria-controls` causes AT to ignore the attribute. Pre-existing from 13-s2.
- **D-13S3-CR2: `w-[calc(100vw-3rem)]` undocumented width utility** — `LumiWhisper.tsx`: AC3 specifies only `max-w-xs`; the extra intrinsic-width utility makes the widget expand to nearly full viewport on narrow screens. Better UX in practice; low risk.
- **D-13S3-CR3: "See why" opens on the stored surface, not the nudge's surface** — Inherent in `summon()` with no surface argument; if a nudge arrived for a different surface while the sheet was open (and was not appended due to mismatch), "See why" will show unrelated content. Needs a dedicated `summon(surface)` call with the nudge's surface.
- **D-13S3-CR4: No runtime guard on `pendingNudge.body` being undefined** — `LumiWhisper.tsx:14`: if upstream contract is violated and `body` is undefined, `body.type` throws. Upstream contract responsibility.
- **D-13S3-CR5: No axe E2E assertion for whisper state (AC5)** — The 13-s1 baseline spec predates this slice and does not enter `presenceState='whisper'`; AA coverage for LumiWhisper is unit-only. Not a regression.
- **D-13S3-CR6: `setNudge(null)` in dismiss clears a pointer already appended to the thread** — Semantic coupling between `pendingNudge` (the "surface" pointer) and `turns` (the appended thread entries). Clearing `pendingNudge` after append is safe (the data is in `turns`), but the coupling is non-obvious.

- **D-13S2-CR1: Stale turns after surface switch while summoned** — `LumiSheet.tsx:96`: `turnsNow.length > 0` guard prevents hydration for the new surface when old surface's turns are still in the store. Fix requires `setContext()` to clear `turns` (or scope turns per surface). [`LumiSheet.tsx:96`]
- **D-13S2-CR2: Concurrent text submit + active VAD unguarded** — `LumiSheet.tsx:49`: no guard on `voiceStatus === 'active'` before sending a text turn; two concurrent POSTs to `/v1/lumi/turns` can race, making turn order in the UI dependent on promise resolution order rather than `server_seq`. Pre-existing pattern from LumiPanel. [`LumiSheet.tsx:49`]
- **D-13S2-CR3: 401 near abort fires unnecessary auth refresh round-trip** — `LumiSheet.tsx:103`: `hkFetch`'s 401 handler invokes `tryRefreshSession()` before the abort signal propagates, issuing two extra auth network requests after the user has navigated away. Pre-existing behavior in `hkFetch`. [`lib/fetch.ts`]

- **D-13S1-CR1: Confirm-week button is an inert no-op in production** — `BriefCanvas.tsx:689` renders `<PlanActionSection>` with no `onConfirm`/`onTalkToLumi`, so the "Confirm the week" button's `onClick` is `undefined` and permanently inert. AC1.3's visible+enabled assertion is satisfied but the baseline pins a confirm surface that does not confirm; a rebuild could ship the same dead button and pass. Pre-existing product issue, not a 13-s1 defect. [`apps/web/src/features/plan/BriefCanvas.tsx:689`; `PlanActionSection.tsx:21-27`]
- **D-13S1-CR2: Axe a11y baseline does not scan the opened LumiPanel/picker, and the orb/panel `aria-expanded`/`aria-controls` contract is unasserted** — `checkA11y(page,'.app-scope',…)` runs only on the default brief view; the panel textarea, mode toggles, and DisambiguationPicker are outside the a11y gate the story claims protects "ambient LumiOrb/Panel." Coverage enhancement (AC2 as written is met); no `src/` change needed to extend. [`apps/web/test/e2e/13-s1-ux-regression-baseline.spec.ts:269-276`]
- **D-13S1-CR3: PlanTile `past`/`today`/`locked` visual variants uncovered after AC3 snapshot descope** — only `paused` is covered behaviorally (AC5); the other PlanTile states AC3 enumerated have no behavioral substitute. Consequence of the user-approved pixel-snapshot descope; flag for a later behavioral or visual-regression slice. [`apps/web/src/features/plan/PlanTile.tsx`]

## Deferred from: code review of 2.7-s7-pure-turn-fn-delete-safety-nets-break-up-god-method (2026-06-27)

- **D-2.7S7-CR1: `m2AnsweredThisTurn` advances M2 on any non-empty message in `m2_safe`** — allergen safety relies on the chip-driven UI, not a server-side content gate. Any API call with free text in `m2_safe` advances the controller with zero allergen data. Intentional user-approved design tradeoff (AskUserQuestion #1). [`onboarding.service.ts` — M2 slot signal]
- **D-2.7S7-CR2: Prior-turn M3 chip signal checks message-key presence, not confirmed DB write** — `m3ChipPriorTurn` finds an M3 chip key in the turn body but cannot verify the `dietary.declare`/`cuisine.declare` call succeeded. A prior tool failure advances the FSM with no taste preferences persisted. Pre-existing pattern identical to the deleted M3 auto-advance safety net. [`onboarding.service.ts:m3ChipPriorTurn`]
- **D-2.7S7-CR3: Prior-turn M4 chip signal has the same message-key-only gap** — `m4ChipPriorTurn` does not verify a successful `child.upsert`; M4 tool failure → children have no bag pattern but FSM advances. Pre-existing. [`onboarding.service.ts:m4ChipPriorTurn`]
- **D-2.7S7-CR4: M4 pure-chip: `chipKeys[0]` wins silently when multiple M4 keys submitted** — M4 mode is 'action' (single-select); client enforces. A tampered/malformed client with multiple M4 keys silently drops all but the first. Already captured as D-2.7S5-CR4. [`onboarding-turn-runner.ts:applyPureChipTurn m4_bag branch`]
- **D-2.7S7-CR5: 'none' allergen chip writes no DB row — sticky flag only** — If the moment state row is deleted (reset/corruption), `child_allergen_count=0` and the sticky `m2_allergen_response=false`; the controller re-asks allergens. Design tradeoff; moment state row deletion is not a normal path. [`onboarding-turn-runner.ts:applyPureChipTurn m2_safe branch`]
- **D-2.7S7-CR6: `m5ProjectionFailed` void-swallowed in `assembleChipConfig` with no inline log** — The failure IS logged inside `runM5Projection`; the `void` in the helper is a moved pre-existing silent no-op. [`onboarding.service.ts:assembleChipConfig`]
- **D-2.7S7-CR7: M3 'skip' key absent from `M3_CHIP_KEYS` — `m3ChipPriorTurn` misses prior-skip turns** — On session resume with `preTurnMomentState=null`, the controller's `reconstructMoment` re-anchor (m1+m2 done → m4) handles M3-skip without `m3Answered`; only a moment-state-row-deletion edge is affected. [`onboarding.service.ts:m3ChipPriorTurn`]
- **D-2.7S7-CR8: Repeated `request_ratification=true` calls can persistently block `m3Answered`** — Each turn where a tool result has `ratification_requested=true` sets `ratificationRequested=true`, which forces `m3Answered=false` regardless of prior committed M3 chips. Requires model to persistently misbehave; `m3ChipPriorTurn` would break the cycle once the model stops. [`onboarding.service.ts:m3Answered`]
- **D-2.7S7-CR9: Slot-signal assembly left in service rather than extracted to controller** — `controllerSlots` construction (M2/M3/M4/M5 signals) remains in `submitTextTurn`, leaving the service with more than "thread bookkeeping only" responsibility (partial AC3 deviation). Dev record acknowledges: regression risk > decomposition benefit at this stage. [`onboarding.service.ts:submitTextTurn moment block`]

## Deferred from: code review of 2.7-s6-slot-model-onboarding-controller (2026-06-27)

- **D-2.7S6-CR1: History scan for m3Answered/m4Answered has no moment guard** — the `existingTurns` scan in the shadow slot assembly checks for M3/M4 chip keys across all prior turns with no filter on which moment those turns occurred in. A reset segment that previously captured M3 chips, or a corrupted turn submitted in the wrong moment, would permanently mark m3Answered=true. Safe in shadow mode (controller not authoritative); must be scoped by moment before s7 deletes the LLM-led path. [`apps/api/src/modules/onboarding/onboarding.service.ts` — shadow block]
- **D-2.7S6-CR2: Shadow log covers moment divergences only; chip_config divergences are not shadowed** — the `onboarding.controller_shadow_mismatch` log compares `controllerPredictedMoment` vs `nextCurrentMoment` (transitions only). The AC3 live flip (slot-derived M1 chip swap) is not shadow-compared against the old `[CHIP_PROMPT:household_name]` sentinel path. A regression in `chipConfig` would be silent until golden eval or UI tests caught it. Mitigated for the 9 documented scenarios by the s1 eval. [`apps/api/src/modules/onboarding/onboarding.service.ts`]
- **D-2.7S6-CR3: MOMENT_SLOT_PREDICATES.m5Complete name collides with OnboardingSlots.m5Complete field** — the predicate key `m5Complete` and the interface field `m5Complete` share a name; renaming one without the other silently breaks the predicate. Not a runtime bug today (predicate is tautologically `(s) => s.m5Complete`). [`apps/api/src/modules/onboarding/onboarding.controller.ts:56`]
- **D-2.7S6-CR4: nextMoment silently returns unrecognised CurrentMoment values unchanged** — if DB stores a corrupted or future-added moment key not in the FSM's if-chain, `nextMoment` returns it unchanged with no log or throw. TypeScript union types guard at compile time but not against DB-sourced strings. Pattern is pre-existing in the codebase; only becomes a real risk when the controller is authoritative in s7. [`apps/api/src/modules/onboarding/onboarding.controller.ts:84`]

The handler validates via VocabularyService at the tool boundary, so invalid keys produce an error in `summary` (not a panic), but the moment still advances (see D-2.7S5-CR1 general note on silent errors). The ratification detection logic executes correctly (it's post-agent), but the pure-chip path is not covered. `onboarding-zero-call.test.ts` does wire all deps and covers the production path. - **D-2.7S4-CR6: `finishReason === 'stop'` exits the tool loop even when tool calls are present** — pre-existing OR logic (`toolCalls.length === 0 || response.finishReason === 'stop'`); in practice OpenAI never emits tool calls simultaneously with finish_reason=stop, but the guard is logically over-broad. Faithfully mirrors the established `handleAddAllergen` idiom (same latent issue across all optimistic kitchen-profile handlers); concurrent multi-child editing is low-likelihood. ## Deferred from: code review of 5-s15-voice-transcript-retention-controls (2026-06-08)

If the profile update fails after the delete succeeds, transcripts are permanently gone but `voice_retention_mode` stays `standard`. Client reverts optimistically, briefly showing phantom transcripts until next page load. Spec explicitly accepts this as "acceptable MVP behavior" (delete-before-update is the safer failure mode — data was cleared, which the user asked for). [`apps/api/src/modules/users/user.routes.ts`]

They are invisible via `findByUserId` (correct API behavior) but not deleted on mode change. The nightly `deleteExpired` purge cleans them at `retention_until`. By-design per spec reconciliation note (no backfill possible — no user association exists for pre-migration rows). [`apps/api/src/modules/voice/voice-transcript.repository.ts`, `supabase/migrations/20261023000000_add_voice_retention_mode.sql`]

- **`pendingProposal` is single-slot — a second proposal erases the first day's pulse** — multi-day concurrent proposals aren't modeled; proposing on Wed wipes Mon's pending signal. Fold into D-5S12-1 when modeling resolution. [`apps/web/src/features/plan/BriefCanvas.tsx:135,493`]
- **Idempotency-Key required but not honored (no replay cache)** — pre-existing repo-wide pattern; `plans.routes.ts:50-51` notes "Full Redis replay-cache deferred." All 9 plan routes validate format only. A retry with the same key still appends a new proposal turn. [`apps/api/src/modules/plans/plans.routes.ts:304`]
- **`createThread` unique-violation race → unhandled 500** — narrow first-proposal window (two concurrent requests when no active family thread exists); `ThreadRepository` exposes `isUniqueViolation` but the route doesn't map it. Low severity. [`apps/api/src/modules/plans/plans.routes.ts:306-313`]
- **Picker re-targeted day→day without remount keeps stale L3 state** — `<DisambiguationPicker>` has no `key={activeSwapDay}`, so switching the open day in place preserves `level`/`proposalInput`/`error`. Pre-existing class (the `l3-variation-ingredients` `ingredientInput` shares it). A `key` prop fixes both. [`apps/web/src/features/plan/BriefCanvas.tsx:560`]

## Deferred from: code review of 5-s10-cultural-recognition-family-language-ratchet (2026-06-08)

- **R0: Threshold crossing consumed before the prompt turn is durably created** — `recordUsage` persists `usage_count >= threshold` first; if the subsequent `insertTurn` throws (caught + swallowed best-effort), the one-shot `prev < threshold <= next` crossing never recurs, so the parent is never prompted and the term sits at `candidate` forever (never injected into the snapshot). Deferred: rare failure path (a DB write must fail in the gap between count-save and card-creation); tied to the spec's deliberate no-`prompted`-flag simplification. Fix = re-derive the crossing idempotently (`usage_count >= threshold && state==='candidate' && !prompted`). [`apps/api/src/modules/family-language/family-language.repository.ts:61`]
- **R1: `getTerms` unchecked JSONB cast (no per-element validation)** — a malformed stored element yields `NaN` usage_count (`undefined + n`), which fails the threshold comparison and is written back, permanently poisoning the element. No `FamilyLanguageTermSchema` parse on read. Deferred: the column is new with a `'[]'` default, so no malformed data can exist yet; add a defensive `safeParse`-and-skip on read if the shape ever changes. [`apps/api/src/modules/family-language/family-language.repository.ts:361-370`]
- **R2: `recordUsage` write amplification for terminal-state terms** — every text turn mentioning an already-`active`/`forgotten` term triggers a households SELECT + full-row UPDATE purely to bump a counter no code reads post-ratification, and each write is also a lost-update window (widens the concurrency risk). No early-out for "all detected terms terminal". Deferred: spec explicitly sanctioned lock-free read-modify-write at beta volume; this is an optimization, not a correctness bug. [`apps/api/src/modules/family-language/family-language.repository.ts` — `recordUsage`]

Consider a minimal "new household" placeholder when onboarding completion tracking is available. [`apps/api/src/modules/lumi/lumi.service.ts:218-241`]

## Deferred from: code review of 7-s13-payload-scrubbing-primitive (2026-06-05)

- **D-1: Shallow scrub — no caller enforcement for nested PII** — `scrubForSharing` is shallow-only by design; a future sharing route that passes a nested child object (e.g. `{ child: { name, declared_allergens } }`) silently passes all nested PII through. The spec delegates this to callers ("provide a pre-flattened payload"), but no mechanism enforces it. Add a call-site pre-flatten requirement (comment, lint rule, or wrapper type) when the first sharing route is built. [`apps/api/src/modules/compliance/payload-scrubber.ts`]
- **D-2: No test for shallow-only boundary behavior** — the test suite has no case confirming that a nested object whose child key matches a sensitive field name is preserved intact (not recursively scrubbed). Spec defines exactly 5 cases; this boundary test is out of scope for 7-S13. Add when a sharing route story builds on this primitive. [`apps/api/src/modules/compliance/payload-scrubber.test.ts`]
- **D-3: Case-sensitive Set matching — camelCase/mixed-case variants bypass scrubbing** — `Set.has('childName')` is false; any serializer or ORM that outputs camelCase field names would silently pass sensitive data through. Add a normalization step (e.g. `key.toLowerCase()` or a camelCase-to-snake_case transform) before the membership check if a camelCase sharing surface is ever built. [`apps/api/src/modules/compliance/payload-scrubber.ts`]
- **D-4: Array-of-records input unguarded** — `Record<string, unknown>` excludes arrays at the TypeScript level, but a caller-side cast or `JSON.parse` path can bypass the type check; the function would return an object with numeric indices, not a scrubbed array. Add an overload or array-mapping helper when the first array-shaped sharing route is built. [`apps/api/src/modules/compliance/payload-scrubber.ts`]
- **D-5: Null input causes runtime TypeError** — `Object.entries(null)` throws `TypeError: Cannot convert undefined or null to object`; TypeScript's type excludes null but untyped call paths (agent tool output, route body) can still pass it. Add a `if (payload == null) return {}` guard if untyped call paths emerge. [`apps/api/src/modules/compliance/payload-scrubber.ts`]

- **D-1: Non-existent `householdId` silently returns `[]`** — `maybeSingle()` returns `{data:null}` when household not found; code falls through to `stateResidency=null → return []`, masking a bad ID at the call site. No 404 behavior. Add a `if (!hRow) throw new NotFoundError(...)` guard when Epic 8 wires the first caller. [`apps/api/src/modules/compliance/compliance.repository.ts`]
- **D-2: `households.state_residency` has no DB CHECK constraint** — column is `TEXT` with no `CHECK (char_length(state_residency)=2 AND state_residency ~ '^[A-Z]{2}$')`. Invalid values bypass Zod validation on direct DB writes. Add constraint in Epic 8's `setStateResidency` migration. [`supabase/migrations/20260613000000_state_residency_scaffold.sql`]
- **D-3: `state_compliance_overrides.state` has no DB CHECK constraint** — `state TEXT NOT NULL` with no format guard; lowercase or long values silently return zero overrides. Add CHECK alongside D-2. [`supabase/migrations/20260613000000_state_residency_scaffold.sql`]
- **D-4: `override_type` is unconstrained free text** — no DB enum or `z.enum()` in the contract; future branching on this field will break on typos. Lock down to a known enum once Epic 8 defines the first override_type value. [`packages/contracts/src/state-compliance.ts` / migration]
- **D-5: TOCTOU between two-step household+overrides queries** — `state_residency` could be updated concurrently between the two awaits, returning overrides for the wrong state. Resolve by converting to a single JOIN when Epic 8 wires the route. [`apps/api/src/modules/compliance/compliance.repository.ts`]
- **D-6: `today` uses Node.js UTC clock** — `new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10)` is always UTC; a US household at midnight may get the wrong calendar day. Document as UTC-anchored or derive from the DB's `NOW()::DATE` at route wiring time. [`apps/api/src/modules/compliance/compliance.repository.ts`]
- **D-7: Repository mock doesn't assert `.eq()` argument values** — `householdId` and `stateResidency` passed to queries are never validated; a bug passing the wrong value would not be caught. Add `expect(_val).toBe(HOUSEHOLD_ID)` assertions to the mock when test coverage is deepened. [`apps/api/src/modules/compliance/state-compliance.repository.test.ts`]
- **D-8: No composite index on `(state, effective_from)`** — the query filters on both columns; the single-column index on `state` will degrade to a secondary in-memory filter at scale. Add `CREATE INDEX ... ON state_compliance_overrides (state, effective_from)` in a follow-up migration. [`supabase/migrations/20260613000000_state_residency_scaffold.sql`]
- **D-9: No `effective_to` column** — retiring an override requires DELETE with no audit history of when it was active. Add `effective_to DATE` + filter `AND (effective_to IS NULL OR effective_to > today)` when the first real override_type is defined. [`supabase/migrations/20260613000000_state_residency_scaffold.sql`]
- **D-10: Two sequential DB round-trips** — household lookup + overrides query could be a single JOIN, eliminating the TOCTOU window and one network hop. Optimize when Epic 8 wires the route. [`apps/api/src/modules/compliance/compliance.repository.ts`]

## Deferred from: code review of 7-s10-json-data-export (2026-06-05)

- **D-1: Unbounded export payload size** — no size guard before `JSON.stringify(snapshot)` + Supabase Storage upload; a large household could produce a payload exceeding the 50 MB bucket limit and exhaust all 3 BullMQ retry attempts with no user notification. Pre-existing beta-scale design constraint; no spec requirement for a guard. [`apps/api/src/jobs/data-export.job.ts`]
- **D-2: Email re-sent on BullMQ retry** — if the job fails after storage upload succeeds (e.g. getUserById transient error or after P1 fix — skip email on no-email, audit write fails), a retry re-uploads the same file and re-sends email. User may receive 2–3 emails. Known limitation of the on-demand job pattern; no dedup mechanism specified in AC5. [`apps/api/src/jobs/data-export.job.ts`]
- **D-3: DEK-null throws in `findAllForHousehold` if household DEK row deleted** — if `household_keys` row for the household is deleted independently of the KEK env var, `getHouseholdDek` returns null and `decryptField` throws on the first encrypted note, causing 3× job retries with no user notification. Same exposure as existing `listByHousehold`. Not introduced by this story. [`apps/api/src/modules/heart-notes/heart-note.repository.ts`]
- **D-4: HMAC signature verification complexity** — `signature = HMAC-SHA256(JSON.stringify(snapshot), JWT_SECRET)` but the stored file is `JSON.stringify({data: snapshot, signature}, null, 2)`. A verifier must extract `.data` and re-serialize with `JSON.stringify` to reproduce the signed bytes — key ordering and whitespace must match exactly. No verification procedure is specified in the spec; latent trap if verification tooling is ever built. [`apps/api/src/jobs/data-export.job.ts`]
- **D-5: Dead `(household ?? {})` null-coalesce** — `getHousehold` calls `.single()` which throws on zero rows; the `?? {}` fallback is unreachable. Harmless dead code but slightly misleading about whether `household` can be null. [`apps/api/src/jobs/data-export.job.ts:69`]

Guest authors are invited household members (low risk), and the story explicitly scopes the bypass as the "Save for next month" UX feature. ## Deferred from: code review of 4-s6-scheduling-edit-cancel-delivery-status (2026-05-28)

- **Delivery job uses wall-clock date — stranded if delayed past midnight UTC** — `heart-note-delivery.job.ts` computes `isoDate = new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10)` at runtime. If the BullMQ job is delayed past midnight UTC (queue backlog, worker restart), notes scheduled for the intended date are never delivered and remain permanently `'scheduled'`. The spec prescribes this implementation; a fix requires passing the scheduled date on job data. [`apps/api/src/jobs/heart-note-delivery.job.ts`]
- **`patch()` calls `getOrCreateHouseholdDek` for content mutations** — Semantically, a PATCH on an existing note should never mint a new DEK (the note was already created under one). If the DEK were somehow absent, the repo silently creates a new one, leaving previously encrypted rows unreadable. The correct call is `getHouseholdDek`. Risk is theoretical since DEK deletion would require out-of-band DB admin action. [`apps/api/src/modules/heart-notes/heart-note.repository.ts:patch`]
- **Empty PATCH body issues spurious UPDATE with `updated_at` bump** — When `patchNote` is called with an all-optional-fields body where all are undefined, `repo.patch` builds an empty `update` object and still issues a DB UPDATE, triggering the `updated_at` trigger unnecessarily. Benign but wasteful. [`apps/api/src/modules/heart-notes/heart-note.repository.ts:patch`]
- **Test mock `buildHeartNotesSelectChain` fragile eq-count disambiguation** — The mock routes `findById` (2 `.eq()` calls) vs `findByChildAndDate` (3 `.eq()` calls) by counting invocations. Adding a fourth `.eq()` to any method, or re-ordering eq calls, silently routes to wrong mock data. [`apps/api/src/modules/heart-notes/heart-note.routes.test.ts:buildHeartNotesSelectChain`]
- **`handleCopyLink` not gated on note existence** — The "Copy Link" button calls `/v1/lunch-link/generate` without checking `noteIdRef.current` or `draft !== null`. A user can generate and copy a valid lunch-link URL before writing any note content, resulting in a link that opens with `heartNote: null` on the child side. [`apps/web/src/routes/(app)/heart-note.tsx:handleCopyLink`]
- **`patchNote` allows past-date `scheduled_for` on a scheduled note — permanently stranded** — Setting `scheduled_for` to a past date on a `'scheduled'` note leaves it permanently in `'scheduled'` status (the delivery job for that past date has already run). The schema validates format only (`z.string().date()`), not future-date constraint. Address with a `refine(d => d >= today)` in a future slice. [`apps/api/src/modules/heart-notes/heart-note.service.ts:patchNote`, `packages/contracts/src/heart-notes.ts:PatchHeartNoteBodySchema`]

## Deferred from: code review of 3-24-allergy-uncertainty-flagging-safe-substitution (2026-05-24)

- **[⚠ SAFETY] FALCPA synonym rename breaks onboarding chip key synonym expansion** — 2.6-s7 renamed engine canonical keys (`peanuts→peanut`, `milk→dairy`, `eggs→egg`, `tree_nuts→tree_nut`) but the onboarding M2 chip set still emits the old plural forms as the stored allergen key (e.g. `allergen: 'eggs'`). `targetsFor('eggs')` returns only `['eggs']` because `FALCPA_SYNONYMS['eggs']` no longer exists; the canonical `'egg'` synonym list (mayo, albumin, aioli, meringue, etc.) is never applied. A child declared as allergic to `'eggs'` via onboarding will not be protected against egg-derived ingredients. Fix: update onboarding chip keys to canonical singular forms OR add plural-form aliases directly to `FALCPA_SYNONYMS`. Pre-existing from 2.6-s7; surfaced by 3-24 review. [`apps/api/src/agents/tools/onboarding.tools.ts` — M2 chip keys]
- **`AllergyUncertaintyBanner` list key includes array index** — the React `key` prop combines `childName|day|slot|ingredient|idx`; including the index causes re-mount on item reorder and silently shows duplicate flagged-item rows when the same `(childName, day, slot, ingredient)` tuple appears more than once. Dedup before render or remove the `idx` tiebreaker. [`apps/web/src/features/plan/AllergyUncertaintyBanner.tsx:1465`]
- **Phantom compound-flagged tuples cause wasted swap LLM call** — when `compoundFlagged` contains a `(child_id, day, slot)` tuple absent from `previousCommit.items`, `buildBlockedItems` silently drops it but `blockedKeys` retains the phantom key. The subsequent coverage check finds the uncovered key and falls back to full `planWeek`, consuming a mini-tier swap call for zero benefit. Fix: intersect `blockedKeys` with the actual resolved `blockedItems` after `buildBlockedItems` returns. [`apps/api/src/jobs/swap-retry.helper.ts:97`]
- **Deferred-work `planWeek()` param count says "8" but actual is 12** — the deferred-work entry was updated from "7" to "8" per spec instruction, but the real signature had 11 params before 3.24 and 12 after. The nominal count is misleading for the options-object refactor planning window. Update to reflect the actual count when the refactor ships. [`_bmad-output/implementation-artifacts/deferred-work.md`]
- ~~**AC7 banner not wired to live plan data**~~ **Partially resolved by Story 3.25 (2026-05-25)**: the audit-row lookup pattern (`GET /v1/plans` consults a `plan.hard_fail` audit row, surfaces `hard_fail` on the response, frontend renders `<AccountableError />`) is now wired end-to-end. The `<AllergyUncertaintyBanner>` integration that surfaces compound-uncertain `flagged_items` to the parent on the safe-substitution path is still deferred — `generation_failure` field on `GetPlansResponseSchema` + audit-metadata population in `plan.generation.failed` (with `reason` / `flagged_items`) remain to be done. [`apps/web/src/features/plan/AllergyUncertaintyBanner.tsx`, `packages/contracts/src/plan.ts`, `apps/api/src/modules/plans/plans.routes.ts`]

## Deferred from: implementation of 3-24-allergy-uncertainty-flagging-safe-substitution (2026-05-24)

- **Compound-uncertain swap uses prompt instruction, not structural merge** — `trySurgicalSwap` synthesizes a `BlockedItem` per compound-flagged slot with `allergen: 'unknown_compound'` and prepends an `ALLERGEN-UNCERTAIN: …` line to the swap agent's context; the planner is expected to choose single-ingredient replacements. The deterministic guardrail re-evaluates the merged plan, so a non-compliant planner produces a re-flagged plan, not an unsafe one. A structural merge layer (planner output → guarantee single-ingredient items for flagged tuples) is the next-step hardening if telemetry shows compound-substitution non-compliance. Same posture as the 3.23 slot-scope deferred entry. [`apps/api/src/jobs/swap-retry.helper.ts: trySurgicalSwap`, `apps/api/src/agents/orchestrator.ts: swapBlockedItems`]
- **"Mark as safe" household-level compound override** — no `household_compound_overrides` table introduced. If a parent declares "my garam masala is safe — never flag it for my household," the engine has no way to record or honour that opinion; the slot will keep getting flagged on each plan generation. Deferred to a later story when product validates that the substitution path produces enough friction to justify the override surface. [`apps/api/src/modules/allergy-guardrail/`]
- **Frontend wiring for `<AllergyUncertaintyBanner>` is component-only** — the banner ships as a standalone, prop-driven React component (`apps/web/src/features/plan/AllergyUncertaintyBanner.tsx`). The plan-view integration (and the supporting `generation_failure?: { reason, flagged_items }` field on `GetPlansResponseSchema` + the route handler that reads from `plan.generation.failed` audit rows with `metadata.reason === 'compound_ingredient_unverified'`) is a follow-up: it spans the contract, the API plans route, the audit lookup, and the PlanPage hook. The component is integration-ready; AC7's live render depends on the follow-up landing. [`apps/web/src/features/plan/AllergyUncertaintyBanner.tsx`, `packages/contracts/src/plan.ts: GetPlansResponseSchema`, `apps/api/src/modules/plans/plans.routes.ts`]
- **`generation_failure` (compound-uncertain `reason` / `flagged_items`) not yet written to `plan.generation.failed` audit metadata** — Story 3.25 added `is_guardrail_rejection: boolean` so ops can distinguish guardrail-retry exhaustion from infra failures, and emits a `plan.hard_fail` audit row with full per-attempt `stages` (carrying compound-uncertain `reason` / `flagged_items`) before the throw. The frontend-facing `<AllergyUncertaintyBanner>` surfacing path (rejection payload propagated into `plan.generation.failed.metadata` + `generation_failure?` field on `GetPlansResponseSchema`) is still deferred — that banner targets the user-recoverable compound-uncertain case, not the hard-fail terminal state. [`apps/api/src/jobs/plan-generation.job.ts: generationWorker.on('failed', …)`, `apps/api/src/modules/plans/plans.service.ts: commit`]
- **`evaluateSnackSku()` does NOT participate in compound detection** — the structured-flag path used by Snack SKUs (Story 3.20) bypasses the `evaluate()` ingredient loop entirely; compound detection ONLY fires for items that pass through `evaluate()`. SKU items by definition have known FALCPA flags and no compound provenance ambiguity, so this is correct today — but if `snack_skus` ever grows to include compound products (sauces, blends), the engine will fail-open. Document the model boundary; revisit if SKU catalog scope expands. [`apps/api/src/modules/allergy-guardrail/allergy-rules.engine.ts: evaluateSnackSku`]

- **`main()` in lint script calls `getTypeScriptFiles` twice (double readdir + TOCTOU)** — the "N files scanned" count in the OK message is computed from a second independent `readdir` call, introducing a race if files are created between the two calls. Minor accuracy issue. [`apps/api/scripts/check-sacred-channel-boundary.ts:main`]
- **`decryptField` auth-tag failures surface with no row/household context** — when GCM decryption fails (wrong key or bit corruption), the thrown error has no `household_id`, `child_id`, or note `id` attached. Pino captures the request ID but not which row triggered the failure, making DB-level corruption hard to trace. [`apps/api/src/modules/heart-notes/heart-note.repository.ts`]

## Deferred from: code review of 2.5-s3-chip-turn-ux-primitive (2026-05-20)

- **SkipChip onClick is a no-op stub** — deferred to 2.5-s4 by design; `chip_config` is hardcoded null in this slice so SkipChip never renders. Real skip behavior (submit a skip turn, advance agent to next moment) lives in the 2.5-s4 prompt. [`apps/web/src/features/onboarding/OnboardingText.tsx:941-945`]
- **Zod union order silently drops `chip_selections` on mixed-key body** — `z.union([TextTurnBodySchema, ChipTurnBodySchema])` evaluates text branch first; a body containing both `message` and `chip_selections` matches text, stripping chip data silently. Fix would be `.strict()` on `TextTurnBodySchema` or a discriminated union. Low practical risk since the client never sends mixed keys. [`packages/contracts/src/onboarding.ts:33-36`]
- **Chip key strings injected verbatim into agent message without allowlist** — `chip_selections` values are bounded to 64 chars each but not validated against server-emitted options. Design choice per spec: "Do NOT try to do anything fancier here." Revisit when adding server-side chip option persistence. [`apps/api/src/modules/onboarding/onboarding.routes.ts:103`]
- **Stale chipSelections after 502 rollback when chipConfig changes between turns** — 502 rollback restores the pre-send chip state snapshot. If the retry succeeds and returns a new `chipConfig` with different options, the restored `chipSelections` may contain keys that don't exist in the new option set and get serialized into the next turn's agent message. Only relevant post-2.5-s4 when the agent starts emitting non-null chip configs. [`apps/web/src/features/onboarding/OnboardingText.tsx:745-753`]
- **Orphaned optimistic turn on AbortController fire** — pre-existing: rapid re-submit aborts the first in-flight request, which exits its catch via `AbortError` without removing the first optimistic turn from `turns` state. The ghost user turn persists alongside the second optimistic turn until the page reloads. [`apps/web/src/features/onboarding/OnboardingText.tsx:704`]
- **Double-fire pending guard race on rapid submit** — pre-existing: two synchronous submit events fired before the first `setPending(true)` propagates through React's scheduler both see `pending = false` and proceed. [`apps/web/src/features/onboarding/OnboardingText.tsx:673-674`]
- **ChipConfigSchema allows mode/field mismatch** — `mode: 'hint'` with `options` set (or `mode: 'action'` with `hints`) passes schema validation. The renderer handles this gracefully but the contract is weaker than it should be. A `z.discriminatedUnion('mode', [...])` with per-mode shape would self-enforce. Future refactor. [`packages/contracts/src/onboarding.ts`]

## Deferred from: kitchen-map persistence audit during Epic 2 onboarding work (2026-05-13)

- **~~No `recipes` table exists~~ — CLOSED 2026-05-14** by `supabase/migrations/20260514010000_create_recipes_and_usage.sql`. The migration adds `recipes` (with FALCPA allergen flag pre-computation mirroring `snack_skus`), `household_recipe_usage` (per-(household, recipe) confidence + favourites + counters), `recipe_comments` (with a `recipe_comments_public` view for anonymous display), and retrofits `plan_items.recipe_id` with a real FK (existing values NULL'd as part of the migration — they pointed nowhere). Recipes are populated at plan-commit time by RecipesService (to-be-built). Replaces the stub `RecipeService.search/fetch` `NotImplementedError`s with a real backing table.
- **`RecipeService.search()` and `RecipeService.fetch()` both throw `NotImplementedError`** — the service is a stub. The `recipe.search` and `recipe.fetch` agent tools in `recipe.tools.ts` (with Zod schemas + latency budgets of 300ms / 100ms) wrap these stubs; calling them in production throws. The orchestrator unit tests only pass because they stub the service. Any planner code path that depends on recipe lookup will fail at runtime today. [`apps/api/src/modules/recipe/recipe.service.ts`, `apps/api/src/agents/tools/recipe.tools.ts`, `apps/api/src/agents/orchestrator.test.ts`]
- **"Recipe" content today lives only in `plan_items.ingredients` jsonb** — the LLM synthesizes meal content per generation, persists ingredient strings as a jsonb array on the plan_item row, and the allergy guardrail evaluates against that array (not against a recipe row). No prep steps, method, image URL, prep_time, cuisine tags, or anything else a real recipe library would carry. Re-using a `recipe_id` across plans is not deterministic — the planner doesn't guarantee the same UUID maps to the same dish. [`apps/api/src/modules/plans/plans.service.ts`, `apps/api/src/agents/orchestrator.ts:planWeek`]
- **Implications for Epic 2 (`onboarding-builds-kitchen-map`)** — if the parent mentions a treasured dish during the interview (e.g., *"my mum's biryani"*, *"the Sunday lamb roast"*), there is no `recipes` table to upsert into. The natural home is `memory_nodes` with `node_type='palate'` or a new facet like `'family_dish'`. This is fine for v1 of slice 2-s22 but limits future product moves like "save this recipe", "Lumi suggests we cook the biryani again", or a curated cultural recipe library that seeds new households. [`apps/api/src/modules/memory/memory.service.ts:noteFromAgent`, `_bmad-output/planning-artifacts/epic-2-onboarding-integration.md` slice 2-s22]
- **Roadmap implications** — a future `recipes` table + curated catalog (parallel to `snack_skus`) would unlock: recipe re-use across plans, parent-saved-recipes, deep-links from the brief tile to a recipe view, "make this again" affordances, recipe-level allergen pre-computation (like `snack_skus.contains_*`), and cultural recipe seeds for new households. Not in any current epic's scope; logging here so the gap stays visible when product prioritisation revisits planner / cookbook surfaces.

- **No `plan_item_id` uniqueness guard on `extra_removal_signals`** — rapid back-and-forth swaps of the same plan item can insert multiple rows with the same `plan_item_id`, inflating the rolling 30-day count faster than intended. - **Fire-and-forget signal recording has no retry on transient DB failure** — a Supabase blip during the swap path permanently loses the removal signal; architectural choice documented in spec. Add a dead-letter queue or durable event log if signal reliability becomes a product concern. [`apps/api/src/modules/plans/plans.service.ts`]
- **`applyBias` self-healing loop under persistent `markSignalsApplied` failure** — if `markSignalsApplied` keeps failing, every subsequent swap of the same type burns a round-trip through `applyBias` (already-banned guard fires and retries the flip). Degrades gracefully; no correctness harm. Add a consecutive-failure counter or exponential backoff if this becomes noisy in production. [`apps/api/src/modules/plans/extra-removal-signal.service.ts:applyBias`]

## Deferred from: 3-16-school-policy-update-propagation (2026-05-05)

- **Slot-level partial regeneration not implemented** — when a parent activates a slot-scoped policy (`slot_scope: 'main' | 'snack' | 'extra'`), the service enqueues full **week-scope** regen jobs because `PlanRegenerationJobData.scope` only supports `'week' | 'day'`. The orchestrator can read `slot_scope` from the audit/metadata trail but rebuilds the entire week. Adds: introduce `scope: 'slot'` + `slot` field in regen job + day/slot-aware `commit_plan()` archival path. [`apps/api/src/modules/children/school-policies.service.ts`, `apps/api/src/jobs/plan-regeneration.job.ts`]
- **`plan.updated` SSE for policy-triggered regen also deferred to Story 5.2** — same constraint as 3.13. Until 5.2, clients see policy-driven changes via TanStack Query polling. [`apps/api/src/jobs/plan-regeneration.job.ts`]
- **`policy_description` not user-surfaced** — the contract supports it but `SchoolPoliciesForm` only toggles canonical `policy_type` presets. A free-text input is deferred until UX decides whether free-text policies should affect plan generation (today they do via the legacy `school_policy_notes` column). [`apps/web/src/features/children/SchoolPoliciesForm.tsx`]
- **No regen rate-limit reuse** — Story 3.13's per-household-week Redis rate limit (5/wk) is not consulted when policy changes propagate. A parent rapidly toggling many policies could enqueue dozens of jobs for the same plan within minutes. BullMQ's per-policy `jobId` dedupes within a single request_id but not across requests; revisit if policy-toggle abuse becomes plausible. [`apps/api/src/modules/children/school-policies.service.ts`]
- **`findActiveFuturePlanIds` uses UTC Monday math** — same caveat as Story 3.15 (household timezone is unknown to the backend). Acceptable today; tighten with the same per-TZ backend move. [`apps/api/src/modules/plans/plans.repository.ts`]
- **`findActiveFuturePlanIds` has no ORDER BY** — policy propagation batch processes plans in non-deterministic DB order; add `.order('week_of', { ascending: true })` to guarantee chronological processing and avoid revision conflicts on concurrent upserts. Spotted during Story 3.15 backend review. [`apps/api/src/modules/plans/plans.repository.ts:findActiveFuturePlanIds`]
- **Concurrent two-policy activation race** — activating two different policy types in rapid succession (separate PATCH requests) enqueues two independent full-week regen jobs per future plan; whichever worker finishes last wins, and the other policy's constraints may not appear in the final plan. Mitigation: a household-wide regen-debounce (e.g., 30s Redis TTL before enqueuing) or a merged-policy job shape. Deferred: low frequency in practice. [`apps/api/src/modules/children/school-policies.service.ts`]
- **`current_revision` stale in policy-regen job** — the revision captured at enqueue time may already be superseded if a prior regen job (day-swap, user-initiated) completes before this job runs; `commit_plan()` increments the stale revision, which may not advance the brief's `plan_revision` counter from the client's perspective. Pre-existing race in the regen architecture. [`apps/api/src/modules/children/school-policies.service.ts:119`, `apps/api/src/jobs/plan-regeneration.job.ts`]
- **`togglePolicy` hardcodes `slot_scope: 'bag_wide'`** — ~~If a policy row was originally stored with `slot_scope: 'main'`, toggling it off and on silently resets its scope to `bag_wide`.~~ **Resolved by Story 3.23 (2026-05-24)**: `SchoolPoliciesForm` now exposes a per-policy slot-scope `<select>` (All slots / Main / Snack / Extra) and `togglePolicy` preserves the existing scope on re-activation. [`apps/web/src/features/children/SchoolPoliciesForm.tsx`]

## Deferred from: 3-13-plan-regeneration-request (2026-05-04)

- **plan_items archival growth** — every `commit_plan()` call archives the previous set (sets `replaced_by_plan_id`). Without partitioning or a nightly archival job moving rows older than 90 days to `plan_items_archive`, the table will grow without bound. Acceptable at MVP scale; revisit before scaling. [`supabase/migrations/20260630000200_update_commit_plan_archive_items.sql`]
- **Rate limit counter on failed enqueue** — Redis `INCR` happens before `regenQueue.add()`. A transient queue failure consumes a rate limit slot. Low probability at MVP scale; fix in a follow-up with `MULTI/EXEC` or a compensating `DECR` on enqueue failure. [`apps/api/src/modules/plans/plans.service.ts:requestRegeneration`]
- **`plan.updated` SSE emission for regeneration is deferred to Story 5.2** — `plan-regeneration.job.ts` logs intent but does not emit the SSE event. Client uses 5s polling against `['brief']` while `isRegenerating`. Wire up real SSE emission in Story 5.2 and remove the polling interval from `BriefCanvas`. [`apps/api/src/jobs/plan-regeneration.job.ts`, `apps/web/src/features/plan/BriefCanvas.tsx`]
- **Pre-3.13 plans (week_of = NULL) cannot be regenerated** — the new endpoint returns `ValidationError` (422) when `plan.week_of` is null. After the first automated Friday generation cycle post-deploy, all active plans will have `week_of` populated. No backfill needed; the window is one week. [`apps/api/src/modules/plans/plans.service.ts:requestRegeneration`]
- **`fastify.bullmq.getWorker(REGEN_QUEUE)` cache by name is brittle** — `bullmqPlugin.getWorker(name, processor, opts)` always requires a processor argument; if `getWorker` is called twice for the same queue, the second call's processor is silently ignored. Current code calls it once and stores the worker reference. Document the contract or refactor `BullMQFacade.getWorker` to throw on duplicate registration. [`apps/api/src/plugins/bullmq.plugin.ts`]
- **`isRegenerating` stuck true on permanent BullMQ failure** — no client-side timeout or SSE `plan.generation.failed` signal to clear the spinner. Client polls indefinitely until the user navigates away. Fix requires Story 5.2 SSE fan-out to emit `plan.generation.failed` with `request_id` correlation so `BriefCanvas` can detect the failure and set `isRegenerating(false)`. [`apps/web/src/features/plan/BriefCanvas.tsx`, `apps/api/src/jobs/plan-regeneration.job.ts`]
- **429 regen error is silent to the user** — `handleRegenerate.onError` logs to console but does not surface feedback. `rateLimitRemaining` from the 202 response is also not displayed. Both require a toast/notification system not yet in place; defer to the notification infra story. [`apps/web/src/features/plan/BriefCanvas.tsx:handleRegenerate`, `apps/web/src/features/plan/mutations.ts`]
- **`isRegenerating` resets on unmount; remount after navigation captures already-bumped revision as baseline** — `lastPlanRevisionRef` is component-local; navigating away and back while regen is in-flight causes the revision-detection effect to re-baseline at the bumped value, so the spinner never clears on remount. Fix requires persisting `isRegenerating` + `lastPlanRevisionRef` in a Zustand store so state survives navigation; deferred to Story 5.2 with SSE. [`apps/web/src/features/plan/BriefCanvas.tsx`]
- **Concurrent household member action (swap/pause) can bump `plan_revision`, prematurely clearing `isRegenerating`** — the detection heuristic compares `brief.plan_revision > lastPlanRevisionRef.current` without correlating the bump to the regen `request_id`. A secondary caregiver swap while regen is pending will appear to resolve the spinner early. Proper fix requires server-sent `regen.complete` event with `request_id`; deferred to Story 5.2. [`apps/web/src/features/plan/BriefCanvas.tsx`]

- **Same-revision concurrent brief writes can race on swap/pause path** — composer is invoked synchronously from HTTP handlers, not via BullMQ; per-household serialization that 3-11 documented does not apply here. Last writer wins. [`apps/api/src/modules/plans/brief-state.repository.ts:upsert`]

Composer-must-not-throw is a contract invariant; surfacing the failure would change that contract. [`apps/api/src/modules/plans/plans.service.ts:swapItem`]
- **No invalidation of `['brief']` on mutation `onError`** — transient swap failure leaves the optimistic tile in `swap-in-progress` until next stale-time refetch. Add `onError: () => queryClient.invalidateQueries(['brief'])`. [`apps/web/src/features/plan/mutations.ts`]
- **`isAllergenAffecting` substring match has UX-only false positives/negatives** — server is authoritative. Comment claims "false positives are safe" but glosses false negatives. Update comment to clarify this is an optimistic-vs-pending UX heuristic, not a safety gate. [`apps/web/src/features/plan/DisambiguationPicker.tsx`]
- **`isAllergenAffecting` not Unicode-normalized** — "Café" vs "cafe" mismatch; add `.normalize('NFKD')` for i18n. [`apps/web/src/features/plan/DisambiguationPicker.tsx`]
- **DisambiguationPicker `<div role="group">` `onKeyDown` ESLint suppression** — same a11y pattern as 3-10/3-11 popovers; address in codebase-wide a11y pass. [`apps/web/src/features/plan/DisambiguationPicker.tsx`]
- **Empty-string allergen in `child.declared_allergens` would produce unparseable brief response** — composer should defensive-filter at write time. Latent (no current path emits empty allergens). [`apps/api/src/modules/plans/brief-state.composer.ts`]
- **Migration ordering** — code references `paused_at` in `PLAN_ITEM_COLUMNS` already; deploying code before running the migration breaks every `findItemsByPlanId`. Document in deploy runbook.
- **`SwapPlanItemInputSchema` cannot clear a previously-non-null `recipe_id`** — no nullable + no clear-sentinel. API design gap. [`packages/contracts/src/plan.ts:SwapPlanItemInputSchema`]
- **Picker error state persists across L3↔L2 navigation** — Back buttons don't clear `error`. [`apps/web/src/features/plan/DisambiguationPicker.tsx`]
- **Picker `Enter` on empty L3 input fires submit then errors** — double key path. [`apps/web/src/features/plan/DisambiguationPicker.tsx`]
- **Picker comma-only input briefly enables Swap button** — `","`.length === 1 passes the disabled check. [`apps/web/src/features/plan/DisambiguationPicker.tsx`]
- **Picker Escape handler doesn't `stopPropagation`** — bubbles to ancestor dialogs/modals. [`apps/web/src/features/plan/DisambiguationPicker.tsx`]
- **Mid-mutation unmount race** — `onSwapStarted`/`onSwapSettled` may fire on unmounted parent. React 19 warning only. [`apps/web/src/features/plan/DisambiguationPicker.tsx`]
- **`SwapPlanItemResponseSchema` returns `paused_at`** — service does not clear it on swap. Inconsistent with response shape; will be fixed by the paused-swap patch in this review. [`apps/api/src/modules/plans/plans.service.ts:255-261`]
- **`brief_state.plan_id` FK `ON DELETE SET NULL`** — client assumes `plan_id` always populated when items exist. Plan deletes not expected at MVP. [`supabase/migrations/20260621000200`]
- **`PlanItemRowSchema.ingredients` allows `[]` but `SwapPlanItemInputSchema.ingredients` requires `min(1)`** — empty stored item is uneditable via the only swap mechanism. [`packages/contracts/src/plan.ts`]
- **`hkFetch` won't overwrite caller-supplied `Authorization` when `accessToken === null`** — defensive; no current callers do this. [`apps/web/src/lib/fetch.ts`]
- **Idempotency-Key UUID regex accepts non-v4 UUIDs despite comment** — cosmetic. [`apps/api/src/modules/plans/plans.routes.ts:requireIdempotencyKey`]
- **`pauseDay` test asserts `expect.any(String)` for `pausedAt`** — weak assertion. [`apps/api/src/modules/plans/plans.repository.test.ts`]

Per-child pause requires extending the contract and UI; revisit when household composition + sibling-day-mismatch use cases land. [`apps/api/src/modules/plans/plans.repository.ts`, `apps/api/src/modules/plans/brief-state.composer.ts`]
- **SSE fan-out is a stub; cross-tab swap visibility waits for explicit refetch** — after a successful swap/pause, the mutating client invalidates `['brief']`; other connected tabs (secondary caregiver) only see the update on focus/staleTime expiry. - Integration test `if (app) await app.close()` contradicts `let app: FastifyInstance` non-nullable declaration [apps/api/test/integration/plugins.int.test.ts] — runtime safe; tighten to `let app: FastifyInstance | undefined` when integration story resumes.
- `vitest.config.ts` include has redundant glob (`test/**/*.test.ts` already matches `.int.test.ts`) — Vitest dedupes; cosmetic.
- Vitest coverage reporter omits `lcov` — add when CI coverage aggregation story lands.
- `ELEVENLABS_WEBHOOK_SECRET` / `STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET` required but unused by Story 1.6 — developers populate dummies that would later pass signature checks vacuously; revisit `.min()` / format when webhook stories land.
- No Supabase service-role-key liveness check on startup [apps/api/src/plugins/supabase.plugin.ts] — invalid/revoked keys surface only at first query. Startup probe story.
- Extensive unrelated working-tree changes (`packages/ui/*`, `apps/web/*`, `_color-gen.mjs`, `packages/eslint-config-hivekitchen/*`) outside Story 1.6 scope — tracked for separate PRs or prior-story rollups. Appears to include uncommitted Story 1.5 artifacts.
- Plugin registration ordering not type-enforced (a future reorder past `app.decorate('env', env)` would give SDK plugins `undefined`) [apps/api/src/app.ts:27-30] — Fastify pattern limitation; no feasible type-level guard.
- No global `unhandledRejection` / `uncaughtException` handlers routing through Pino [apps/api/src/server.ts] — deferred to Story 1.7 (Pino structured logging / OTEL skeleton scope).

## Deferred from: code review of 1-10-realtime-sse-bridge-central-invalidationevent-dispatcher (2026-04-24)

- `client_id` echo suppression at the server — Story 5.2; without it, optimistic-mutation echoes can race with local state.
- Server-side `thread.turn` deduplication, reordering, and cached-array cap — Story 5.x; the bridge appends faithfully per AC #2 and trusts server contract for ordering.

- `queryClient.clear()` on logout to evict cached PII (child names, allergies, heart notes) — auth flow not yet present (Story 2.2).
- Server graceful drain on SIGTERM for long-lived SSE connections (Fastify `app.close()` will hang on open SSE handlers) — operational, post-Story 5.2.
- Rate limit / connection cap per IP on `/v1/events` — Story 5.x operational hardening; current stub allows unbounded anonymous connections.
- `audit-hook` `onResponse` fires when the SSE stream closes — recorded request duration is the entire connection lifetime, skewing dashboards. Surfaces with Story 5.2 / Epic 9.

- `apps/web/vitest.config.ts` uses `__dirname` instead of `import.meta.url` + `fileURLToPath` — tooling-config drift; the project invariant targets `src/` files. Low risk; tidy in a tooling-hygiene pass.

## Deferred from: implementation of 2-1-supabase-auth-email-password-google-apple-oauth (2026-04-25)

- Live `pnpm supabase:reset` verification of the three new migrations was skipped because Docker Desktop was not running on the dev machine. Migrations are direct copies of the spec; next dev with Docker should run `npx supabase start && pnpm supabase:reset && pnpm seed:dev` and confirm `\d users`, `\d households`, `\d refresh_tokens`, and `SELECT typname FROM pg_type WHERE typname='user_role';` succeed. [`supabase/migrations/`]
- Live `pnpm dev:api` smoke (with real Supabase env values) was not performed in this story; only `pnpm typecheck` validates env-schema parsing. Verify before any deploy that all six new env keys (SUPABASE_ANON_KEY, four OAuth credentials, WEB_BASE_URL) are populated in `.env.local`. [`apps/api/.env.local.example`]
- The `users.role` column defaults to `'primary_parent'` on insert, but only the bootstrap `create_household_and_user` RPC explicitly sets it. Story 2.3 (secondary-caregiver invite) needs to override this default at user creation time — document the override path when wiring 2.3. [`supabase/migrations/20260501120000_create_users_and_households.sql`]
- `auth.service.logout()` revokes the HK refresh-token row but does NOT call `supabase.auth.signOut({ scope: 'global' })` for the underlying Supabase session. The Supabase JWT issued by `signInWithPassword` is therefore still valid until its natural expiry (1 h default) — a hostile-cookie scenario could replay it against Supabase directly. Acceptable for 2.1 because the API is the only consumer, but Story 2.2 should call `auth.admin.signOut(supabase_session_token, 'global')` once we persist the Supabase session token alongside the HK refresh row. [`apps/api/src/modules/auth/auth.service.ts`]
- `setRefreshCookie` reads `env.NODE_ENV !== 'development'` for the Secure flag — this means `staging` and `test` get Secure (correct for staging, accidentally broken for `test` if any future browser-based test runs against an http test server). Add a `secure: env.NODE_ENV === 'production' || env.NODE_ENV === 'staging'` guard if test breakage emerges. [`apps/api/src/modules/auth/auth.routes.ts`]
- The frontend `/` route currently `Navigate`s to `/auth/login` for everyone — once Story 2.2 adds `authenticate.hook.ts` and a session-bootstrap query, swap this for an "if authed → /app, else → /auth/login" guard. The current redirect is intentional for 2.1 because no client-side session detection exists yet. [`apps/web/src/app.tsx`]
- `apps/web/src/lib/supabase-client.ts` constructs the client at module-load even when the user never reaches the login page (e.g., direct deep-link to a public marketing route once Epic 11 lands). Consider lazy initialization if cold-start bundle size becomes a concern. [`apps/web/src/lib/supabase-client.ts`]
- The fastify-type-provider-zod validation-array branch in the global error handler hand-rolls a "ZodError-like" pseudo-issue array. If `fastify-type-provider-zod` v5+ changes the validation shape, this branch silently drifts. Replace with a direct call to the library's documented error-mapping helper if/when one ships. [`apps/api/src/app.ts`]
- **Discovered during 2.1 live smoke** (out of 2.1 scope, pre-existing from Story 1.8): `apps/api/src/jobs/audit-partition-rotation.job.ts` declares `QUEUE_NAME = 'audit:partition-rotation'`. BullMQ v5 rejects queue names containing `:` ("Queue name cannot contain :") because Redis keys are colon-separated internally — this prevents `pnpm dev:api` from booting. Rename to `audit-partition-rotation` (and update any operator dashboards/queries that reference the old name) in a Story-1.8 follow-up. [`apps/api/src/jobs/audit-partition-rotation.job.ts`]

- `updateUser` Zustand action silently no-ops when `state.user` is null (e.g., concurrent logout during a slow PATCH) — acceptable silent behaviour for this race. [`apps/web/src/stores/auth.store.ts`]
- `/v1/auth/` blanket `SKIP_PREFIXES` entry is an implicit convention — any future route added under `/v1/auth/` is unauthenticated by default without any explicit marker. Architectural doc concern. [`apps/api/src/middleware/authenticate.hook.ts`]
- Supabase mock chain in tests hardcodes method-chaining order — column name changes or query restructuring pass tests undetected. Accepted test-mock pattern in codebase. [`apps/api/src/modules/users/user.routes.test.ts`]
- Password-reset test does not assert that no auth token is required — nice-to-have assertion to confirm public-route intent. [`apps/api/src/modules/users/user.routes.test.ts`]

- **OAuth → `is_first_login` → onboarding redirect silently discards invite** — if a new user signs up via OAuth while following an invite link, `callback.tsx` redirects to `/onboarding` on `is_first_login: true`, permanently discarding the `?next=/invite/:token` destination. ## Deferred from: code review of 2-5-notification-preferences-cultural-language-preference (2026-04-26)

- Service-layer read-modify-write on `notification_prefs` has no row lock — concurrent PATCHes from the same user both read the same stale row, compute divergent merged objects, and the last write wins, silently discarding the first write. Fix requires DB-side `jsonb_set` (atomic JSONB merge) or `SELECT ... FOR UPDATE` row locking in the repository. [`apps/api/src/modules/users/user.service.ts:95-107`]
- Ratchet check for family-language is service-only — not enforced by a DB CHECK constraint. Two concurrent forward-move PATCHes that both read `cultural_language = 'default'` both pass the guard and proceed to write; the 'default'-reversal protection also has this gap. Add a DB-level trigger or CHECK if the ratchet must be unconditionally durable. [`apps/api/src/modules/users/user.service.ts:121`]
- `UnauthorizedError` (401) for user-not-found after a valid JWT — semantically incorrect; should be 404 or 403. Pre-existing pattern throughout `user.service.ts` (`getMyProfile`, `updateMyProfile`, `updateMyNotifications`, `updateMyPreferences`). [`apps/api/src/modules/users/user.service.ts:96`]
- `updateMyPreferences` writes to the DB even when the input value exactly matches the current stored value (`fieldsChanged` = `[]`). No early-exit guard results in an unnecessary DB write + `fetchAuthProviders` Admin API call. [`apps/api/src/modules/users/user.service.ts:130`]

Operational mitigation: secret rotation policy + secret-storage hygiene; defense-in-depth via the `agent_id` verification patch (review item #14). Sequential webhook processing avoids the race today, but Story 5.x (concurrent text-mode appends to shared family thread) will hit the `UNIQUE (thread_id, server_seq)` constraint. Fix path: Postgres RPC or per-thread sequence with default expression. [`apps/api/src/modules/voice/voice.repository.ts`]
- **`extractSummary` accepts unbounded transcript length** — joins entire transcript and sends to gpt-4o without max-token clipping. Onboarding's 10-minute budget caps real-world size, low risk for this story. Add token-clipping when text-mode (Story 2.7) and longer threads land. [`apps/api/src/agents/onboarding.agent.ts`]
- **Real Supabase integration test for `UNIQUE (thread_id, server_seq)` collision** — current vitest mocks return `{ error: null }` regardless of duplicate `server_seq`, so the race-condition contract is unenforced by tests. Needs Supabase test-DB infra. [`apps/api/src/modules/voice/voice.routes.test.ts`]
- **Scoped content-type parser isolation test** — no test asserts that the `parseAs: 'string'` parser scoped to the webhook route does NOT bleed into other JSON routes. A regression that globalised the parser would not be caught. [`apps/api/src/modules/voice/voice.routes.test.ts`]

## Deferred from: Story 2-6b voice pipeline v2 (2026-04-28)

- **In-memory WsSession store** — `VoiceService.sessions` is a `Map<string, WsSession>`. At
  beta scale (150 concurrent HH) this is sufficient. At 5,000+ HH scale, WS connections must
  be routable to a specific API instance or the map must be replaced with Redis. Ticket: upgrade
  VoiceService to Redis-backed session store when API is deployed behind a load balancer.

- **ElevenLabs Scribe async mode** — Scribe v1 is synchronous REST (~200–400ms). ElevenLabs
  provides an async batched Scribe endpoint with lower per-word cost. Evaluate when voice
  usage exceeds 100 HH active simultaneously.

- **MediaSource streaming audio** — current implementation buffers all MP3 chunks and plays
  on response.end. MediaSource API would reduce perceived latency by ~200ms. Deferred until
  there is user feedback that the pause before Lumi speaks is noticeable.

- **JWT `?token=` in access logs** — `GET /v1/voice/ws?token=<jwt>` is logged by Fastify's
  request serialiser with the full query string. The bearer token is valid for 15 minutes and
  is written to any log-aggregation system (Datadog, CloudWatch, etc.). Fix: add a pino request
  serialiser in `app.ts` that redacts `?token=<value>` to `?token=REDACTED`.
  [`apps/api/src/app.ts`, `apps/api/src/modules/voice/voice.routes.ts:69`]

- **`submitTextTurn` race-recovery conflates "finalized" and "not found"** — when a
  `createThread` unique-violation is caught and the winner's thread is re-read as `null`
  (because it was immediately finalized), the code throws `ConflictError('onboarding already
  complete')`. If the null is instead due to Supabase read-after-write lag on a replica, the
  error message is factually wrong and the client has no signal to retry. Fix: after the re-read
  returns `null`, call `householdHasCompletedOnboarding` before choosing the error message.
  [`apps/api/src/modules/onboarding/onboarding.service.ts:96-107`]

- **Orphaned user turn detection false-positive on duplicate short messages** — `submitTextTurn`
  treats a trailing user turn as an orphan when `lastTurn.body.content === input.message`. Short
  confirmation messages ("Yes", "OK") sent twice in legitimate succession are silently
  de-duplicated; the second message reuses the old `turn_id`. Fix: add a guard that only marks a
  turn as orphaned when it has no following lumi reply AND was persisted recently (e.g., within
  the last 30 s). [`apps/api/src/modules/onboarding/onboarding.service.ts:136-141`]

- **VAD onnxruntime-web WASM not served in production build** — `@ricky0123/vad-react@0.0.36`
  depends on `onnxruntime-web` which fetches `.wasm` binaries at runtime. `vite.config.ts` has
  no WASM configuration (`vite-plugin-wasm`, `optimizeDeps.exclude`, or `assetsInclude`) and
  `apps/web/public/` contains no WASM files. Vite dev server may serve the files via module
  resolution, masking the issue in development. Test: `pnpm --filter @hivekitchen/web build &&
  vite preview` and open the voice onboarding step — if the WASM fetch fails the VAD will not
  initialise and the mic will never trigger. Fix: copy the required WASM files from
  `node_modules/onnxruntime-web/dist/*.wasm` to `apps/web/public/` and configure
  `ortConfig.wasmPaths = '/'` in `useMicVAD` options, OR add `vite-plugin-wasm` to
  `vite.config.ts`. [`apps/web/vite.config.ts`, `apps/web/src/hooks/useVoiceSession.ts`]

- **`@elevenlabs/elevenlabs-js` SDK client is dead code** — `elevenlabs.plugin.ts` decorates
  `fastify.elevenlabs` with an `ElevenLabsClient` instance, but no call site in the codebase
  accesses `fastify.elevenlabs`. All ElevenLabs API calls in `voice.service.ts` use raw
  `fetch()` with the API key passed as a string through `VoiceServiceDeps`. Fix: either remove
  `elevenlabs.plugin.ts` and the `@elevenlabs/elevenlabs-js` dependency (keeping raw fetch), or
  migrate `VoiceService` to call methods on the SDK client and remove the raw fetch calls.
  [`apps/api/src/plugins/elevenlabs.plugin.ts`, `apps/api/src/modules/voice/voice.service.ts`]

Valid proxy today because `findCurrentByHousehold` gates on `guardrail_cleared_at IS NOT NULL` — only agent-guardrail-cleared plans reach the composer. Long-term fix: during agent tool execution, store explicit per-(child, allergen, item) verdicts; brief-state composer reads stored verdicts instead of inferring cleared status. New story needed: **"guardrail verdict persistence per plan item"** — covers schema (new `plan_allergen_verdicts` table or `plan_items.guardrail_cleared_allergens text[]` column), agent tool layer (write verdict at commit time), and composer update (replace `buildClearedAllergies` with a verdict read). [`apps/api/src/modules/plans/brief-state.composer.ts:151`]

## Deferred from: code review of 3-13-plan-regeneration-request (2026-05-04)

- **SSE `plan.updated` not emitted from regeneration job** — AC #2 requires it; client uses 5s TanStack Query polling instead. Wire up real SSE emission in Story 5.2 and remove the polling interval from BriefCanvas. [`apps/api/src/jobs/plan-regeneration.job.ts`]
- **Rate-limit INCR before job enqueue consumes token on failed enqueue** — Redis INCR fires before `regenQueue.add()`; a transient queue failure burns a rate-limit slot. Fix with a Redis MULTI/EXEC transaction pairing INCR with job confirmation. [`apps/api/src/modules/plans/plans.service.ts — requestRegeneration`]
- **Non-atomic INCR + EXPIRE — process crash between the two Redis calls leaves a no-TTL key, permanently rate-limiting the household** — use `SET key 1 EX ttl NX` + `INCR` Lua script or `redis.pipeline()` to make both ops atomic. Related: when TTL=-1 (key has no expiry), the 429 `Retry-After` fallback returns `REGEN_TTL_SECONDS` (8 days) — misleading because the key will never expire; fix TTL repair logic alongside atomicity fix. [`apps/api/src/modules/plans/plans.service.ts — requestRegeneration`]
- **Client retry with same `Idempotency-Key` runs a fresh Redis INCR even though BullMQ deduplicates the job** — a household that retries once (network blip) burns 2 rate-limit slots for a single actual regeneration. Fix: check Redis for an existing jobId before INCR, or move INCR inside the enqueue transaction. [`apps/api/src/modules/plans/plans.service.ts — requestRegeneration`]
- **Route-level tests for `POST /v1/plans/:planId/regenerate` are absent** — `plans.routes.test.ts` has zero coverage for the new endpoint: 202 body shape, 429 + `Retry-After` header, `scope=day` Zod validation rejection, missing `Idempotency-Key` header. Service-layer tests cover the logic thoroughly but HTTP-layer regression coverage is missing. [`apps/api/src/modules/plans/plans.routes.test.ts`]
- **Rate-limit TTL is rolling 8 days from first use, not calendar-aligned to Sunday midnight** — a household who first regenerates on Thursday carries the counter into the next week; architectural note says "expires Sunday midnight". Fix: compute seconds until next Sunday midnight as the TTL. [`apps/api/src/modules/plans/plans.service.ts — requestRegeneration`]
- **Day-scope regen silently un-pauses paused days on other days** — `PlanItemWrite` has no `paused_at` field; other-day items are re-inserted with `paused_at = NULL`, undoing any user-set sick-day pause. Fix requires extending `PlanItemWrite` schema + updating `commit_plan` RPC to accept and preserve `paused_at`. New story needed: "preserve paused state across regeneration". [`apps/api/src/jobs/plan-regeneration.job.ts:110-120`, `packages/contracts/src/plan.ts — PlanItemWriteSchema`]
- **Concurrent regen workers for same plan produce last-write-wins race** — two jobs enqueued rapidly can both commit `revision = N+1` via `ON CONFLICT DO UPDATE`; the second silently discards the first result. Fix requires a distributed lock keyed on `plan_id` in the worker, or a CAS check in `commit_plan`. Story 5.x scope. [`apps/api/src/jobs/plan-regeneration.job.ts`]
- **`REGEN_JOB_OPTS` defined in job file but duplicated inline at enqueue call site** — divergence risk if one is updated without the other; `failed` handler's maxAttempts guard falls back to the file constant which may not match. Fix: export `REGEN_JOB_OPTS` and import it in `plans.service.ts`. [`apps/api/src/jobs/plan-regeneration.job.ts:26-31`, `apps/api/src/modules/plans/plans.service.ts`]
- **`week_of VARCHAR(10)` lacks a DB-level CHECK constraint** — Zod enforces `YYYY-MM-DD` at the API boundary; a direct SQL insert or admin script can write arbitrary 10-char strings. Add `CHECK (week_of ~ '^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$')` or change to native `DATE` type in a future schema hardening pass. [`supabase/migrations/20260630000000_add_week_of_to_plans.sql`]
