## Deferred from: code review of 2.5-s4 — pre-existing architectural concern (2026-05-20)

- **`allergy_rules` FALCPA seed rows universally block 9 allergens for all households — table should be retired** — The `allergy_rules` table was seeded with 9 FALCPA rows (`household_id=NULL, child_id=NULL`) which the guardrail engine evaluates as universal blocks for every child in every household. This means milk, eggs, wheat, soy, fish, shellfish, peanuts, tree nuts, and sesame are excluded from every meal plan regardless of whether any parent declared those allergens. Since 2-s27, parent-declared allergens live in `households.declared_allergens` and `children.declared_allergens` (encrypted). The `allergy_rules` table should be retired. Fix requires: (1) remove FALCPA query from `allergy-guardrail.repository.ts`, replacing it with the parent-declared-only synthetic path; (2) replace `falcpa_baseline_missing` engine check with a meaningful substitute; (3) migration to drop FALCPA seed rows or the whole table; (4) update story 3-24 spec which references this table. Affects: `apps/api/src/modules/allergy-guardrail/allergy-guardrail.repository.ts`, `apps/api/src/modules/allergy-guardrail/allergy-rules.engine.ts`, `supabase/migrations/`, `_bmad-output/implementation-artifacts/3-24-allergy-uncertainty-flagging-safe-substitution.md`. **Must be resolved before Epic 3 plan generation ships to users.**

## Deferred from: code review of 7-s5-soft-hard-promotion-job (2026-06-04)

- **Scheduler-registration failure is logged-and-swallowed → deletion job can silently never run** — `void queue.upsertJobScheduler(...).catch(log)` only logs `scheduler.registration.failed`; if Redis is briefly unavailable at boot the nightly hard-delete sweep never schedules while the app reports healthy, retaining forgotten PII past the 30-day window. This is the established codebase-wide job pattern (`heart-note-delivery.job.ts`, catalog jobs, `audit-partition-rotation.job.ts` all do the same), so it is a cross-cutting ops concern rather than a 7-S5-specific defect. Recommend a shared fix: alert on `scheduler.registration.failed` logs and/or add a startup scheduler health-check across all nightly jobs. [`apps/api/src/jobs/memory-forget.job.ts`]
- **No batching/pagination on the delete → PostgREST `db-max-rows` may leave deleted-but-un-audited nodes at scale** — `hardDeleteSoftForgotten` deletes all matching rows server-side (correct) but returns them in one `.select()`; if a >~1000-node backlog of expired soft-forgotten nodes accumulates, Supabase's `db-max-rows` cap can truncate the *returned* set, so only the first N get a `memory.hard_forgotten` audit row (AC#2 completeness gap). The spec explicitly scoped out pagination ("No pagination. Process all eligible rows per run. Acceptable at beta scale"), and the gap is dormant at current volume. Revisit with a `limit`+loop before scale — which also bounds the otherwise-unbounded synchronous audit loop. [`apps/api/src/modules/memory/memory.repository.ts`]

Minor ordering nit. [`apps/web/src/hooks/useLumiVoiceSession.ts:235`]
- **Non-`message` Lumi turn → silent dead-air in voice** — when the API returns a `lumi_turn` whose `body.type !== 'message'`, `lumiText` is `''`, so nothing is spoken (no `sendToTts`) and `TurnRow` renders nothing; the user gets silence with no cue Lumi acted. Decision (Menon, 2026-06-05): accept silence at MVP — non-message turns are rare in voice. Revisit if action/tool turns become common in voice sessions. [`apps/web/src/hooks/useLumiVoiceSession.ts:160-165`]

## Deferred from: code review of 5-s4-thread-sequencing-sse-resync (2026-06-07)

- **D1: `getNextSeq` string+1 arithmetic may produce `"51"` instead of `6`** — `row.server_seq` typed as `number` but Supabase-js returns PostgreSQL `bigint` columns as strings; `"5" + 1 = "51"` (JS string concat, not integer addition). Affects `lumi.repository.ts:198` and likely `thread.repository.ts` equivalently. If this is real, coordination thread server_seq values are lexicographically ordered strings, not integers. Investigate supabase-js v2 bigint serialisation behaviour; if confirmed, fix with `Number(row.server_seq) + 1` or `parseInt(row.server_seq, 10) + 1`. Pre-existing across 12-S8 and coordination thread paths.
- **D2: Cross-household ownership check two-query race in `getThreadTurns`** — threads ownership validated in one query, then `thread_turns` fetched in a second; if thread is deleted between the two, second query returns empty array rather than an error. Pre-existing codebase-wide pattern across multiple repositories. Address with a join or a transaction if thread deletion becomes part of a real-time cleanup flow. [`apps/api/src/modules/lumi/lumi.repository.ts:37-43`]
- **D3: `threadSeqs` Map never evicted** — the closure-level `threadSeqs` Map in `createSseBridge` grows indefinitely; entries for inactive threads are never removed. Memory leak for long-running sessions with many distinct thread IDs. Also: stale cursors from pre-disconnect state persist through reconnect (which is intentional for gap detection). Consider a time-based or size-capped eviction if memory pressure emerges. Pre-existing design from when `threadSeqs` was first introduced. [`apps/web/src/lib/realtime/sse.ts:77`]
- **D4: `from_seq=0` returns all turns uncapped** — the `fromSeq` path has no `.limit()`; `from_seq=0` passes the `/^\d+$/` regex and `.gte('server_seq', '0')` matches every turn in the thread. Authenticated + household-scoped, so not a cross-household risk. At beta scale acceptable; add a cap (e.g., 500) if threads grow into the thousands pre-launch. [`apps/api/src/modules/lumi/lumi.repository.ts:50-58`]
- **D5: In-flight gap recovery merge after `thread.resync` produces unpredictable cache state** — if a `fetchAndMergeMissedTurns` fetch is in-flight when a `thread.resync` invalidates the cache, the async `setQueryData` may overwrite the post-resync refetch result. The eventual refetch from the invalidation should win, but there is a transient inconsistent render. Known limitation of the fire-and-forget design; accept at MVP. [`apps/web/src/lib/realtime/sse.ts:98-129`]
- **D6: Orphaned coordination thread if `appendTurnNext` fails after `createThread`** — the lazy-create pattern in the PATCH /packer handler creates a new coordination thread and then immediately calls `appendTurnNext`; if that fails, the thread exists with no turns. The next successful PATCH will reuse it and emit `server_seq=1`, which may trigger a spurious anomaly on any client that had previously seen turns on a different coordination thread. Pre-existing from 5-S3. [`apps/api/src/modules/households/households.routes.ts:368-415`]
- **D7: `SequenceId` schema defined independently in `events.ts` and `thread.ts`** — duplication without a shared import means the two schemas could diverge under future maintenance. Pre-existing; create a shared `SequenceId` export in `packages/contracts` when refactoring contracts. Pre-existing.
- **D8: `from_seq` values > PostgreSQL BIGINT_MAX produce opaque error** — no upper-bound guard; `from_seq=99999999999999999999` (~10^19, exceeds BIGINT max of 2^63-1 ≈ 9.2×10^18) would cause PostgREST to return an opaque 400/500 rather than a clean validation error. Add an `z.refine` or `BigInt` range guard if this endpoint is ever publicly documented. Negligible at beta scale. [`apps/api/src/modules/lumi/lumi.routes.ts:24-26`]
- **D9: Reconnect + gap scenario untested at E2E level** — AC1's two-tab disconnect/reconnect scenario is architecturally correct (threadSeqs persists across reconnects within the same bridge instance, so prevSeq is compared against the first post-reconnect event), but no Playwright test exercises it. Add a test that simulates DevTools offline → online with a packer assignment in between when E2E coverage of multi-tab coordination is prioritised.

Consistent with the repo-wide non-transactional multi-write pattern; a real fix needs a Postgres RPC. (apps/api/src/modules/households/kitchen-profile-edit.routes.ts)
- Pre-existing unrelated uncommitted edits ride along in the 7-S15 working-tree diff: `apps/web/src/lib/fetch.ts` `setRestored()` call in `tryRefreshSession` + `apps/web/src/stores/auth.store.ts` (auth session-restore). These predate 7-S15 (only the `PUT` method-union addition in fetch.ts is this story's). Commit and review them separately so the 7-S15 PR stays scoped.
